{"forms":[{"form":"elementary matrices","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"When an elementary matrix multiplies a square matrix from the left, it applies to that matrix an elementary row operation homologous to the one that created its own self.
